Use when the user hands over a batch of papers to add (a list, CSV, pasted text, DOIs, arXiv IDs, URLs, or titles) and wants their official BibTeX fetched and staged. Runs under the miaa-bibtex-curate composer or when the user names this stage. Loads miaa-bibtex-core for all rules. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-acquire))
- **miaa-bibtex-complete**: Fill the missing field values of entries, from the venue library and the official pages, and reconcile entries with their official BibTeX. Use when entries are incomplete (missing month, pages, doi, isbn, and so on), when bibcc reports upstream_only, differs, or local_only fields, when an entry needs the fields its venue's other entries have, or when interim fields must be removed. Runs under any composer or when the user names this stage. Loads miaa-bibtex-core. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-complete))
- **miaa-bibtex-core**: The shared invariants for MIAA survey BibTeX and PDF work. Load this before running any other miaa-bibtex skill. It holds the official-BibTeX hard rule, the bibcc-command-line-only rule, the work-directory and run-report conventions, the retrieval ladder, the dependency preflight, the master report-to-action table, the MIAA hard rules, and rule precedence. It performs no task on its own. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-core))
- **miaa-bibtex-curate**: The full from-scratch flow for a batch of papers, composing acquire, complete, verify, and decide. Use when the user hands over a batch of papers to add to a survey's .bib tree and wants the whole job done, with official BibTeX fetched, fields completed, keys and titles decided, entries placed, and everything verified. Loads miaa-bibtex-core and runs the stage skills in order. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-curate))
- **miaa-bibtex-decide**: Decide and set the content the agent must judge, such as citation keys, title case, brace protection, and taxonomy placement. Use to decide or rename a citation key, fix title case, brace protected terms such as acronyms and method names, or choose which taxonomy file an entry belongs in. Scope is tunable by the request, for example only check quotation or only keys. Applies to any .bib file or folder, whether or not bibcc created it. Runs under any composer or when the user names this stage. Loads miaa-bibtex-core. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-decide))
- **miaa-bibtex-doctor**: Maintain the miaa-bibtex skill set against the installed bibcc. Use when bibcc has been updated or a new version is released, or when the user asks to check the set for drift. It reads bibcc surface, the machine-readable command surface, and diffs it against what miaa-bibtex-core documents, flags changed commands, options, report files, and status words, and checks that every skill loads core and restates no rule. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-doctor))
- **miaa-bibtex-library**: Audit and complete the venue library, the shared asset behind complete. Use to seed or import a venue library, fill missing venue records from official pages, reconcile records across editions and venues, promote a survey's project library to the user library, or hand a lab-wide venue upstream. This is asset-driven work on the library itself, not on a single entry. Runs under the miaa-bibtex-refresh composer or when the user names this stage. Loads miaa-bibtex-core. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-library))
- **miaa-bibtex-pdfs**: Download each entry's PDF and save it as CITATION_KEY.pdf, then verify the library. Use when the user wants the PDFs for a batch or tree fetched and named by citation key, when aligning a PDF folder with a .bib, or when renaming reference-manager exports. Keys must be final first. Runs under the miaa-bibtex-curate composer or when the user names this stage. Loads miaa-bibtex-core. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-pdfs))
- **miaa-bibtex-refresh**: Periodic upkeep of an existing tree, composing upgrade, library, and verify. Loads miaa-bibtex-core and runs the stage skills in order. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-refresh))
- **miaa-bibtex-review**: Audit and repair a .bib file or tree that already exists, composing verify, complete, and decide. - **miaa-bibtex-scholar**: Add Google Scholar citation counts to entries, or verify titles against publisher records. Runs when the user names this stage. Loads miaa-bibtex-core. ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-scholar))
- **miaa-bibtex-upgrade**: Promote interim and preprint entries to their published official BibTeX when a venue's proceedings or issue appear. - **miaa-bibtex-verify**: Audit the structure and syntax of entries against the rules and bibcc's reports, and investigate what bibcc could not compare. Scope is tunable by the request, for example only check quotation or only one file. Applies to any .bib file or folder, whether or not bibcc created it. Runs under any composer or when the user names this stage. Load… ([3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex](https://github.com/3hiuwoo/miaa-bibtex/tree/HEAD/skills/miaa-bibtex-verify))

- **microbenchmarking**: Design, run, and review BenchmarkDotNet microbenchmarks with explicit comparison scope, controlled case count, and measured correctness checks. ([visionarycoder/vc.Agentic](https://github.com/visionarycoder/vc.Agentic/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/microbenchmarking) · 2 copies)
- **Microbial Ecologist Expert Agent**: Specialized expert agent operating as a Microbial Ecologist with full theoretical and practical domain reasoning. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-microbial-ecologist))
- **Microbial Physiologist Expert Agent**: Specialized expert agent operating as a Microbial Physiologist with full theoretical and practical domain reasoning. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-microbial-physiologist))
- **microbial-biotransformation-prediction**: Use when when you have chemical structures (SMILES or molecular structure format) and need to forecast what metabolites environmental microbes would produce; particularly when assessing pollutant persistence, environmental fate, or xenobiotic degradation pathways in aquatic and terrestrial settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbial-biotransformation-prediction))
- **microbial-co-occurrence-network-construction**: Use when a species/genus abundance table needs to be converted into a co-occurrence network with objective edge thresholds, module detection, and hub/connector classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbial-co-occurrence-network-construction))
- **microbial-community-richness-analysis**: Use when a QIIME 2/Tourmaline run has produced alpha-diversity outputs (observed_features_group_significance.qzv, alpha_rarefaction.qzv) and you need to compare richness across a metadata grouping or find the rarefaction plateau depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbial-community-richness-analysis))
- **microbial-dynamics**: Microbial population dynamics modeling and analysis. Bacterial growth curve fitting (logistic, Gompertz, Baranyi), Lotka-Volterra community dynamics, Gillespie stochastic simulation, biofilm quantification, CFU enumeration, and genome annotation. For metabolic modeling use cobrapy; for sequence analysis use biopython. ([synthetic-sciences/openscience](https://github.com/synthetic-sciences/openscience/tree/HEAD/backend/cli/skills/biology/microbial-dynamics) · ★4.4k · 9 copies)
- **microbial-ecologist**: Think and work like an expert Microbial Ecologist. Use when a task calls for Microbial Ecologist judgment. Reasons from Vellend assembly (selection, dispersal, drift, diversification), compositional stats (ANCOM-BC2, MaAsLin2, Aitchison), and SILVA/GTDB/EMP workflows; treats kitome contamination, GCN bias, pseudoreplication, SparCC-as-interaction, and PICRUSt2-as-measured-function as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/microbial-ecologist/skills/microbial-ecologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **microbial-ecologist**: Expert-thinking profile for Microbial Ecologist (field / mesocosm / amplicon & metagenomic community ecology): Reasons from Vellend assembly (selection, dispersal, drift, diversification), compositional stats (ANCOM-BC2, MaAsLin2, Aitchison), and SILVA/GTDB/EMP workflows; treats kitome contamination, GCN bias, pseudoreplication, SparCC-as-interaction, and PICRUSt2-as-measured-function as first-class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/microbial-ecologist) · ★13)
- **microbial-ecologist**: Reasons from Vellend assembly (selection, dispersal, drift, diversification), compositional stats (ANCOM-BC2, MaAsLin2, Aitchison), and SILVA/GTDB/EMP workflows; treats kitome contamination, GCN bias, pseudoreplication, SparCC-as-interaction, and PICRUSt2-as-measured-function as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/microbial-ecologist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **microbial-ecologist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for microbial ecologist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/microbial-ecologist))
- **microbial-genome-annotation-expansion**: Use when a MAG or genome FASTA has partial KEGG KO annotations (e.g. from eggNOG-mapper, KAAS, or KofamKOALA) and the goal is to recover missing orthologs via HMM profile searches to expand KEGG Module completeness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-genome-annotation-expansion))
- **microbial-genome-annotation-harmonization**: Use when you have draft metabolic reconstructions (in SBML or standard format) for multiple organisms sampled from the same microbial community and need to produce a single consensus model per organism that reflects only metabolic capabilities agreed upon across the input reconstructions, or when. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbial-genome-annotation-harmonization) · 2 copies)
- **microbial-genome-detection-thresholding**: Use when a mock community with known composition (e.g. ZymoBIOMICS Microbial Community Standard #D6300) and matched public reference genomes are available and you need to quantify how accurately a tool detects which genomes/strains are present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-genome-detection-thresholding))
- **microbial-genome-mining**: Use when paired metabolomics (tandem MS/GNPS) and genomic data exist for microbial isolates, and the goal is to link identified molecules to known or putative novel biosynthetic gene clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microbial-genome-mining))
- **microbial-gwas-association-testing**: Use when a pan-genome gene presence/absence matrix (orthogroups from OrthoFinder or Roary) and a numeric phenotype table (e.g. 3889 metabolomics traits for 182 yogurt isolates) are available and the goal is to associate orthogenes with traits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/microbial-gwas-association-testing))
- **microbial-metabolomics-phage-impact-assessment**: Use when you have normalized peak intensities (with assigned molecular formulas) from FT-ICR MS analysis of treated and control bacterial samples (or environmental microbial communities), grouped by two or more treatment factors (e.g., phage type: HP1, HS2, control; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbial-metabolomics-phage-impact-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **microbial-mining**: Route microbial / genome mining after assembly or binning: BGCs (antiSMASH, BiG-SCAPE), resistomes and ARG context, mobile elements and plasmid systems, ARG–MGE–host linkage, viruses (geNomad, VirSorter2, CheckV), antimicrobial peptides (Macrel), element cycling (METABOLIC, MEBS, FeGenie), DOM/biosurfactant/HC biodegradation (dbCAN, HADEG, BioSurfDB), metabolism (DRAM, gapseq), defense (DefenseFinder, PADLOC), quorum sensing (QSAP), and annotation substrates (Bakta/Prokka). Use when the question is discovery of functional elements, not community tables alone. ([PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills](https://github.com/PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/microbial-mining) · ★7)
- **microbial-network-algorithm-configuration**: Use when configuring FlashWeave's learn_network call for OTU abundance data and you must decide between sensitive/fine-grained vs fast modes, or whether multi-habitat/multi-protocol data requires the heterogeneous (HE) variant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-network-algorithm-configuration))
- **microbial-phage-infection-experimental-design-interpretation**: Use when you have a bacterium-phage infection study with normalized peak intensities from FT-ICR MS across multiple phage treatment groups (minimum 2–3 conditions such as HP1, HS2, control) and sample replicates (n ≥ 6–8 per group), and you need to test whether phage-type factor explains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/microbial-phage-infection-experimental-design-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **microbial-phenotype-data-preprocessing**: Use when a phenotypic trait table contains continuous (numeric) columns that must be binarized before Scoary2 genome-wide association, and uncertain isolates (posterior probability &lt; 0.85) should be excluded rather than forced into a binary label. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/microbial-phenotype-data-preprocessing))
- **microbial-phenotype-description-preprocessing**: Use when raw microbial taxonomic description text files (e.g., the Part_One_111_final dataset) must be segmented and cleaned before character extraction with MicroPIE or a similar NLP pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-phenotype-description-preprocessing))
- **microbial-phenotype-model-benchmarking**: Use when a comparative-genomics phenotype prediction task must select an SVM kernel: input is a COG presence/absence feature matrix with binary trait labels, and the goal is kernel comparison via balanced accuracy to justify a default kernel choice. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-phenotype-model-benchmarking))
- **microbial-phenotype-prediction-from-genome**: Use when you have bacterial nucleotide FASTA assemblies or predicted genes (e.g. population genomes such as unClos_1/unFirm_1) and need phenotype calls for 67 traits to verify or complement a metabolic reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-phenotype-prediction-from-genome))
- **microbial-phenotypic-character-prediction**: Use when sentences from natural-language microbial taxonomic descriptions must be assigned to phenotypic characters (e.g., Gram stain, motility, NaCl optimum) prior to rule-based character-state extraction, or when reproducing MicroPIE's reported SVM cross-validation metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-phenotypic-character-prediction))
- **microbial-physiologist**: Think and work like an expert Microbial Physiologist. Use when a task calls for Microbial Physiologist judgment. Reasons from Monod/chemostat (μ = D), YX/S and Pirt maintenance, Crabtree/overflow, 13C-MFA and FBA, and BMSAB taxonomy; treats OD-as-biomass yield error, FBA-as-measured-flux, washout misread, and portable ms across media as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/microbial-physiologist/skills/microbial-physiologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **microbial-physiologist**: Expert-thinking profile for Microbial Physiologist (wet-lab / chemostat & metabolic- flux physiology): Reasons from Monod/chemostat (μ = D), YX/S and Pirt maintenance, Crabtree/overflow, 13C-MFA and FBA, and BMSAB taxonomy; treats OD-as-biomass yield error, FBA-as-measured-flux, washout misread, and portable ms across media as first- class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/microbial-physiologist) · ★13)
- **microbial-physiologist**: Reasons from Monod/chemostat (μ = D), YX/S and Pirt maintenance, Crabtree/overflow, 13C-MFA and FBA, and BMSAB taxonomy; treats OD-as-biomass yield error, FBA-as-measured-flux, washout misread, and portable ms across media as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/microbial-physiologist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **microbial-physiologist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for microbial physiologist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/microbial-physiologist))
- **microbial-qtl-association-analysis**: Use when host genotype (SNP/SNV matrix) and 16S rRNA microbial abundance data are available and the goal is to find taxa-SNP associations and flag LD SNV blocks via rho estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-qtl-association-analysis))
- **microbial-read-blast-alignment**: Use when small RNA-seq FASTQ reads have been mapped to hg19/hg38 with STAR and an unmapped BAM/FASTA remains, and you need microbial taxon read counts for the four supported taxons archaea, bacteria, fungi and viruses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microbial-read-blast-alignment))
- **microbial-read-classification**: Use when you have unmapped reads (e.g., a STAR UnMapped.bam from human hg19/hg38 alignment of small RNA-seq FASTQ data) and need to profile fragmental microbial RNA content. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microbial-read-classification))
- **microbial-read-retention-quantification**: Use when benchmarking host-depletion tools for precision on non-human reads: you need to measure microbial read retention (reads_out/reads_in) from DWGSIM/PBSIM2-simulated Illumina and ONT reads against Hostile, HRRT, or masked Hostile indexes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-read-retention-quantification))
- **microbial-sequence-characterization**: Use when host-filtered paired-end FASTQ files from RNA-seq data must be assigned taxonomically to detect microbial (bacterial, archaeal, viral) content, e.g. in metagenomic analysis of SSc skin RNA-seq samples like SRR5074528. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-sequence-characterization))
- **microbial-source-tracking-benchmarking**: Use when comparing fecal source tracking tools on shotgun metagenomes with ground-truth source compositions (e.g., spike-in mesocosms), or when deciding which FST tool and tuning parameters to adopt. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/microbial-source-tracking-benchmarking))
- **microbial-taxa-relative-abundance-quantification**: Use when an OTU/species abundance table with taxonomic assignments and sample metadata needs relative-abundance normalization, group-wise mean/SEM summarization, and significance testing between sample groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/microbial-taxa-relative-abundance-quantification))
